        function GetRecords() {

            var $_GET = {};

            document.location.search.replace(/\??(?:([^=]+)=([^&]*)&?)/g, function () {
                function decode(s) {
                    return decodeURIComponent(s.split("+").join(" "));
                }

                $_GET[decode(arguments[1])] = decode(arguments[2]);
            });

            var hival = $_GET["hival"];
            var lowval = $_GET["lowval"];
            var sector = $_GET["sector"];
            var region = $_GET["region"];
            var timev = $_GET["time"];
            if (hival == null && lowval == null) {


                pageIndex++;
                if (pageIndex == 2 || pageIndex <= pageCount) {
                    $("#loader").show();
                    $.ajax({
                        type: "POST",
                        url: "default.aspx/GetHaberler",
                        data: '{pageIndex: ' + pageIndex + ', kategori: 0, sektor:  0,hi: 0,low: 0,timev:0 }',
                        contentType: "application/json; charset=utf-8",
                        dataType: "json",
                        success: OnSuccess,
                        failure: function (response) {
                            alert(response.responseText);
                        },
                        error: function (response) {
                            alert(response.responseText);
                        }
                    });
                }
            }
            else {
                pageIndex++;
                if (pageIndex == 2 || pageIndex <= pageCount) {
                    $("#loader").show();

                        type: "POST",
                        url: "default.aspx/GetHaberler",

                        failure: function (response) {

<script>
          $("img").each(function (index, element) {
              var $element = $(element);
              var imageUrl = $element.attr("data-src");

              //Do your checks here to change the image-url to a smaller one when required
              if (typeof imageUrl !== typeof undefined && imageUrl !== false) {
                  var alan = document.getElementById('1');

                  $element.attr("src", imageUrl + '?width=' + (alan.offsetWidth - 30) + '&quality=80');
              }

          });
</script>

<script>
        function sectormaindlChanged() {
            var sector = document.getElementById("sectormaindl").value;
            var sectormaindlmenu = document.getElementById("sectormaindlmenu");

            if (sector == 6) {
                sector = 5;
            } else
                if (sector == 7) {
                    sector = 6;
                } else
                    if (sector == 9) {
                        sector = 7;
                    }

            sectormaindlmenu.selectedIndex = sector;
            document.getElementById("select2-sectormaindlmenu-container").innerHTML = document.getElementById("sectormaindl").options[sector].text;

        }
        function regionmaindlChanged() {
            var region = document.getElementById("regionmaindl").value;
            var regionmaindlmenu = document.getElementById("regionmaindlmenu");

            regionmaindlmenu.selectedIndex = region - 1;

            document.getElementById("select2-regionmaindlmenu-container").innerHTML = document.getElementById("regionmaindl").options[region - 1].text;



        }

        function timemaindlChanged() {
            var timeval = document.getElementById("timemaindl").value;
            var timesoldlmenu = document.getElementById("timemaindlmenu");

            timesoldlmenu.selectedIndex = timeval;
            document.getElementById("select2-timemaindlmenu-container").innerHTML = document.getElementById("timemaindl").options[timeval].text;




        }

        function sectormaindlmenuChanged() {
            var sector = document.getElementById("sectormaindlmenu").value;

            var sectormaindl = document.getElementById("sectormaindl");

            if (sector == 6) {
                sector = 5;
            } else
                if (sector == 7) {
                    sector = 6;
                } else
                    if (sector == 9) {
                        sector = 7;
                    }


            sectormaindl.selectedIndex = sector;
            document.getElementById("select2-sectormaindl-container").innerHTML = document.getElementById("sectormaindlmenu").options[sector].text;

        }

        function regionmaindlmenuChanged() {
            var region = document.getElementById("regionmaindlmenu").value;

            var regionmaindl = document.getElementById("regionmaindl");

            regionmaindl.selectedIndex = region - 1;
            document.getElementById("select2-regionmaindl-container").innerHTML = document.getElementById("regionmaindlmenu").options[region - 1].text;

        }

        function timemaindlmenuChanged() {
            var timeval = document.getElementById("timemaindlmenu").value;

            var timemaindl = document.getElementById("timemaindl");

            timemaindl.selectedIndex = timeval;
            document.getElementById("select2-timemaindl-container").innerHTML = document.getElementById("timemaindlmenu").options[timeval].text;

        }
    </script>

            document.getElementById("aspnetForm").onkeypress = function (e) {
                var focused = document.activeElement;
                if ((focused.id != "signinbtn")) {
                    var key = e.charCode || e.keyCode || 0;
                    if (key == 13) {
                        //alert("Please select a company listed?");
                        document.getElementById("expbtn").click();
                        e.preventDefault();
                    }
                }
                else {
                    var key = e.charCode || e.keyCode || 0;
                    if (key == 13) {
                        //var signinb = document.getElementById("signinbtn").click();
                        document.getElementById("signinbtn").focus();
                        document.getElementById("signinbtn").click();
                        e.preventDefault();
                    }


                }


            }

        });

<script type="text/javascript">
        function sendEmail() {

            var data = {
                //'Name': $("#in-message").val(),
                //'Company': $("#in-email").val(),
                'Email': $("#in-email").val(),
                'Message': $("#in-message").val()

            };

            var success = false;

            $.ajax({
                type: "POST",
                contentType: "application/json",
                data: JSON.stringify(data),
                url: "default.aspx/SendEmail",
                dataType: "json",
                async: false,
                success: function (data) {
                    console.log(data); // return from webservice
                    success = true;
                    $("#in-email").hide();
                    $("#in-message").hide();
                    $("#successMsg").show();
                    $("#sendmsgBtn").hide();


                },
                error: function (data) {
                    success = false;
                }
            });

            return success;
        }
    </script>

        }

<script>
        function button_click(objTextBox, objBtnID) {

                document.getElementById("signinbtn").focus();
                document.getElementById("signinbtn").click();
            }
        }

<script type="text/javascript">
        // Enter an API key from the Google API Console:
        //   https://console.developers.google.com/apis/credentials
        var apiKey = 'AIzaSyDnHnXfTGi7CcU9Ru24Lb65k4xCSROWbDo';
        // Enter the API Discovery Docs that describes the APIs you want to
        // access. In this example, we are accessing the People API, so we load
        // Discovery Doc found here: https://developers.google.com/people/api/rest/
        var discoveryDocs = ["https://people.googleapis.com/$discovery/rest?version=v1"];
        // Enter a client ID for a web application from the Google API Console:
        //   https://console.developers.google.com/apis/credentials?project=_
        // In your API Console project, add a JavaScript origin that corresponds
        //   to the domain where you will be running the script.
        var clientId = '780671577895-hori51ph4imapovgqo6o72eiudkv805o.apps.googleusercontent.com';
        // Enter one or more authorization scopes. Refer to the documentation for
        // the API or https://developers.google.com/people/v1/how-tos/authorizing
        // for details.
        var scopes = 'email profile https://www.googleapis.com/auth/profile.language.read https://www.googleapis.com/auth/plus.me https://www.googleapis.com/auth/user.birthday.read https://www.googleapis.com/auth/plus.login';
        var authorizeButton = document.getElementById('authorize-button');
        var signoutButton = document.getElementById('signout-button');

        var theSignOutBtn = document.getElementById('signOutBtn');
        var mypanelBtn = document.getElementById('dashBoardBtn');



        var menuLoginBtn = document.getElementById('menuLoginBtn');
        var menuSignUp = document.getElementById('menuSignUp');

        function handleClientLoad() {
            // Load the API client and auth2 library
            gapi.load('client:auth2', initClient);
        }
        function initClient() {
            gapi.client.init({
                apiKey: apiKey,
                discoveryDocs: discoveryDocs,
                clientId: clientId,
                scope: scopes
            }).then(function () {
                // Listen for sign-in state changes.
                gapi.auth2.getAuthInstance().isSignedIn.listen(updateSigninStatus);
                // Handle the initial sign-in state.
                updateSigninStatus(gapi.auth2.getAuthInstance().isSignedIn.get());
                authorizeButton.onclick = handleAuthClick;
                signoutButton.onclick = handleSignoutClick;
                theSignOutBtn.onclick = handleSignoutClick;
            });
        }
        function updateSigninStatus(isSignedIn) {
            if (isSignedIn) {
                authorizeButton.style.display = 'none';
                signoutButton.style.display = 'block';
                theSignOutBtn.style.display = 'block';
                mypanelBtn.style.display = 'block';
                menuLoginBtn.style.display = 'none';
                menuSignUp.style.display = 'none';
                makeApiCall();
            } else {
                authorizeButton.style.display = 'block';
                signoutButton.style.display = 'none';
                theSignOutBtn.style.display = 'none';
                mypanelBtn.style.display = 'none';
                menuLoginBtn.style.display = 'block';
                menuSignUp.style.display = 'block';
            }
        }
        function handleAuthClick(event) {
            gapi.auth2.getAuthInstance().signIn();
            //gapi.auth2.grantOfflineAccess().then(signInCallback);
            return false;
        }
        function handleSignoutClick(event) {
            gapi.auth2.getAuthInstance().signOut();
            window.location = "default.aspx?exc=logout";
            return false;
        }
        // Load the API and make an API call.  Display the results on the screen.
        function makeApiCall() {
            gapi.client.people.people.get({
                'resourceName': 'people/me',
                'requestMask.includeField': ['person.names', 'person.photos', 'person.emailAddresses', 'person.birthdays', 'person.phoneNumbers', 'person.genders']
            }).then(function (resp) {
                var p = document.createElement('p');
                var name = resp.result.names[0].givenName;

                var day = 01;
                var month = 01;
                var year = 1900;


                if (typeof resp.result.birthdays != "undefined") {

                    if (resp.result.birthdays.length >= 1) {
                        var i;

                        for (i = 0; i < resp.result.birthdays.length; i++) {
                            if (typeof resp.result.birthdays[i].date.day != "undefined") {
                                day = resp.result.birthdays[i].date.day;
                            }
                            if (typeof resp.result.birthdays[i].date.month != "undefined") {
                                month = resp.result.birthdays[i].date.month;

                            }
                            if (typeof resp.result.birthdays[i].date.year != "undefined") {
                                year = resp.result.birthdays[i].date.year;
                            }

                        }
                    }
                }

                var gender = "none";
                if (typeof resp.result.genders != "undefined") {
                    if (resp.result.genders.length >= 1) {
                        var i;


                        for (i = 0; i < resp.result.genders.length; i++) {
                            if (typeof resp.result.genders[i].value != "undefined") {
                                gender = resp.result.genders[i].value;
                            }

                        }
                    }
                }


                $.ajax({
                    type: 'POST',
                    url: 'default.aspx/CheckGoogleUser',
                    // Always include an `X-Requested-With` header in every AJAX request,
                    // to protect against CSRF attacks.
                    headers: {
                        'X-Requested-With': 'XMLHttpRequest'
                    },
                    contentType: "application/json; charset=utf-8",
                    success: function (result) {
                        // Handle or verify the server response.
                        if (result.d == "first") {
                            window.location = "mypanel.aspx";
                        }
                    },
                    error: function (result) {
                        // Handle or verify the server response.
                    },
                    processData: false,
                    data: '{idTokenString: "' + gapi.client.getToken().id_token + '", age: "' + day + "/" + month + "/" + year + '" , gender: "' + gender + '" }' //gapi.client.getToken().id_token
                });


            });







        }
    </script>
